Volhynian Deaths, 1876-79 (M. Momose)

This file contains Death records from the Parishes of Volhynia, South Russia for 
the years 1876-79. This information was compiled by Marcia Momose and coworkers 
December 2000 from the St. Petersburg Lutheran Evangelical archives published by 
the LDS.

Please take some time and review the General file in this Volhynia - St. 
Petersburg directory to gain insight into the history and a list of the team 
working on this project.
